First we calculate the scores of \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathfrak{H}\left({\beta}_{1}\right)=0.121,\mathfrak{H}\left({\beta}_{2}\right)=-0.110$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathfrak{H}\left({\beta}_{3}\right)=0.206,\mathfrak{H}\left({\beta}_{4}\right)=0.140$$\end{document}

Then arrange the computed values in descending order.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\sigma \left(1\right)}=\left(\left(0.8, 0.7\right),\left(\mathrm{0.6,0.7}\right)\right),$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\sigma \left(2\right)}=\left(\left(0.9, 0.5\right),\left(\mathrm{0.5,0.8}\right)\right),$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\sigma (3)}=\left(\left(0.8, 0.4\right),(\mathrm{0.6,0.5})\right),$$\end{document}

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\sigma (4)}=\left(\left(0.7, 0.6\right),(\mathrm{0.8,0.7})\right)$$\end{document}

Thus\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWA\left({\beta}_{1}{,\beta}_{2}, . . .,{\beta}_{n}\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$= \left(\begin{array}{c}\left(\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{4}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}},\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{4}{\left(1-{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}\right),\\ \left({\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{4}{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }},{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{4}{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\right)\end{array}\right),$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left(\left(\mathrm{0.8,0.56}\right),\left(\mathrm{0.64,0.66}\right)\right)$$\end{document}

Theorem 2.

(Idempotency) Consider a collection of n number of CFFNs \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }=\left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}, {\theta }_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}\right),({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }},{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }})\right)$$\end{document}, where \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\k{i} =\mathrm{1,2}, \dots , n$$\end{document} where, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\omega = {\left({\omega }_{1}, {\omega }_{2},. . .,{\omega }_{n}\right)}^{T }$$\end{document} be the associated weight vector of \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }$$\end{document} satisfying \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\omega }_{\k{i} }\in \left[\mathrm{0,1}\right]$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }=1$$\end{document}. If \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}={\beta}_{\sigma (j)}$$\end{document} are mathematically identical where \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\sigma (j)}=\left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}, {\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}\right),({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}},{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}})\right)$$\end{document} then,\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWA\left({\beta}_{1}{,\beta}_{2}, . . .,{\beta}_{n}\right)={\beta}_{\sigma (j)}.$$\end{document}

Proof.

Given that \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}={\beta}_{\sigma (j)}$$\end{document} for all \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\k{i} =\mathrm{1,2},\dots ,n$$\end{document} and some \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$j\in \left\{\mathrm{1,2},\dots ,n\right\}$$\end{document} we can deduce that \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}= {\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}$$\end{document}, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}={\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}$$\end{document}, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}= {\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}={\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}$$\end{document}.

Now, let us delve into the mathematical formulation of CFFOWA by using Definition 10,\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWA\left({\beta}_{1}{,\beta}_{2}, . . .,{\beta}_{n}\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$= \left(\begin{array}{c}\left(\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}},\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}\right),\\ \left({\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }},{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\right)\end{array}\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left(\left(\sqrt[3]{1-{\left(1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }}},\sqrt[3]{1-{\left(1-{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}\right), \left({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}^{{\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }},{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}^{{\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\right)\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left(\sqrt[3]{1-\left(1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(j\right)}}^{3}\right)},\sqrt[3]{1-\left(1-{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(j\right)}}^{3}\right)},({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(j\right)}},{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(j\right)}})\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left(\left(\sqrt[3]{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}^{3}},\sqrt[3]{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}^{3}}\right), \left({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}, {\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}\right) \right)=\left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}, {\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}\right),\left({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}},{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}\right)\right)$$\end{document}

Consequently,\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWA\left({\beta}_{1}, {\beta}_{2}, . . . , {\beta}_{n}\right)={\beta}_{\sigma (j)}$$\end{document}

Theorem 3.

(Boundedness) Let \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}^{-}=\left(\underset{\k{i} }{(\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\},\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\})\underset{\k{i} }{,(\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}\underset{\k{i} }{,\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\})\right)$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}^{+}=\left(\underset{\k{i} }{(\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\},\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\})\underset{\k{i} }{,(\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\},\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\})\right)$$\end{document} be the lower and upper bound of the CFFNs \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }=\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}, {\theta }_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}\right),\left({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}, {\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}\right)$$\end{document}, where \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\k{i} =\mathrm{1,2}, \dots , n$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\omega = {\left({\omega }_{1}, {\omega }_{2},. . .,{\omega }_{n}\right)}^{T }$$\end{document} be weight vector associated with \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }$$\end{document} having conditions \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\omega }_{\k{i} }\in \left[\mathrm{0,1}\right]$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }=1$$\end{document}. Then.

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}^{-}\le CFFOWA\left({\beta}_{1}, {\beta}_{2}, . . . , {\beta}_{n}\right)\le {\beta}^{+}$$\end{document}

Proof.

Consider aggregated output through the CFFOWA operator \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWA\left({\beta}_{1}, {\beta}_{2}, . . . , {\beta}_{n}\right)=\left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{\beta}, {\theta }_{\beta}\right),{(\mathpzc{q}}_{\beta}, {\varphi }_{\beta})\right)$$\end{document}. For each \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}$$\end{document}, we possess.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{ {\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}\le {\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\le \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{ {\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\le {\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\le \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow 1-\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\le 1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\le 1-\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}$$\end{document}

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{3}\right\}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}$$\end{document} \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow {\left(1-\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\right)}^{{\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le {\left(1-\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\right)}^{{\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }}$$\end{document} \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow \left(1-\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\right)\le {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le \left(1-\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\right)$$\end{document}

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\le 1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}$$\end{document} \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow \sqrt[3]{\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\} }\le \sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}\le \sqrt[3]{\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}}$$\end{document}

Hence,4.1\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\le {\mathpzc{r}}_{\beta}\le \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}$$\end{document}

Similarly, for each \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}$$\end{document}, we possess\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{ {\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}\le {\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\le \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{ {\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\le {\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\le \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow 1-\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\le 1-{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\le 1-\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}$$\end{document}

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}$$\end{document} \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow {\left(1-\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\right)}^{{\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le {\left(1-\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\right)}^{{\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }}$$\end{document} \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow \left(1-\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\right)\le {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le \left(1-\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\right)$$\end{document}

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}\le 1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}$$\end{document} \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow \sqrt[3]{\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\} }\le \sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}\le \sqrt[3]{\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right\}}$$\end{document}

Hence,4.2\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}\le {\theta }_{\beta}\le \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}$$\end{document}

Moreover,\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}\le {\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\le \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow {\left(\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}\right)}^{{\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le {\left(\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}\right)}^{{\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }}$$\end{document}

4.3\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}\le {\mathpzc{q}}_{\beta}\le \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}$$\end{document}

Furthermore,\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}\le {\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\le \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left({\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow {\left(\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}\right)}^{{\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left({\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le {\left(\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}\right)}^{{\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }}$$\end{document}

4.4\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}\le {\varphi }_{\beta}\le \underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}$$\end{document}

Hence, by comparing relations 4.1, 4.2, 4.3 and 4.4, we obtain that\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}^{-}\le CFFOWA\left({\beta}_{1}, {\beta}_{2}, . . . , {\beta}_{n}\right)\le {\beta}^{+}$$\end{document}

Theorem 4.

(Monotonicity) Consider two collection of n numbers of CFFNs, denoted as \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }=\left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}, {\theta }_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}\right),({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }},{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }})\right)$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }^{\prime}=\left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }^{\prime}}, {\theta }_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }^{\prime}}\right),({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }^{\prime}},{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }^{\prime}})\right)$$\end{document}, with \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\k{i}$$\end{document} ranging from \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$1$$\end{document} to \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$n$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\omega = {\left({\omega }_{1}, {\omega }_{2},. . .,{\omega }_{n}\right)}^{T}$$\end{document} represents the weight vector associated with \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }^{\prime}$$\end{document} satisfies the requirement \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\omega }_{\k{i} }\in \left[\mathrm{0,1}\right]$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }=1$$\end{document}. If \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\le {\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}^{\prime}}$$\end{document}, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\le {\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}^{\prime}}$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\ge {\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}^{\prime}}$$\end{document}, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\ge {\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}^{\prime}}$$\end{document} then we can establish that:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOW\phi\left({\beta}_{1}, {\beta}_{2}, . . . , {\beta}_{n}\right)\le CFFOW\phi\left({\beta}_{1}^{\prime}, {\beta}_{2}^{\prime}, . . . , {\beta}_{n}^{\prime}\right)$$\end{document}

Proof.

The provided presentation of \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }^{\prime}$$\end{document} yields the following outcomes of CFFOWA:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWA\left({\beta}_{1}, {\beta}_{2}, . . . , {\beta}_{n}\right)=\left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{\beta}, {\theta }_{\beta}\right),({\mathpzc{q}}_{\beta},{\varphi }_{\beta})\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWA\left({\beta}_{1}^{\prime}, {\beta}_{2}^{\prime}, . . . , {\beta}_{n}^{\prime}\right)=\left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}^{\prime}}, {\theta }_{{\beta}^{\prime}}\right),({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}^{\prime}},{\varphi }_{{\beta}^{\prime}})\right)$$\end{document}

Since \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\le {\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}^{\prime}}$$\end{document}, which implies that \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\le {\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}^{\prime}}^{3}$$\end{document}, we can deduce that\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\ge 1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}^{\prime}}^{3}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\ge {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}^{\prime}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow 1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\le 1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}^{\prime}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}$$\end{document}

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow \sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}\le \sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}^{\prime}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}$$\end{document}

Hence we can conclude that,4.5\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathpzc{r}}_{\beta}\le {\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}^{\prime}}$$\end{document}

Similarly, by considering \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\le {\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}^{\prime}}$$\end{document} and adopting the above procedure, we obtain the following relation\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}\le \sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}^{\prime}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}$$\end{document}4.6\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\theta }_{\beta}\le {\theta }_{{\beta}^{\prime}}$$\end{document}

Moreover,\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\ge {\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}^{\prime}}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\Rightarrow {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\ge {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}^{\prime}}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}$$\end{document}4.7\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathpzc{q}}_{\beta}\ge {\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}^{\prime}}$$\end{document}

By adopting the above procedure, we obtain the following inequalities4.8\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\varphi }_{\beta}\ge {\varphi }_{{\beta}^{\prime}}$$\end{document}

Therefore, by comparing 4.5, 4.6, 4.7 and 4.8 and utilizing Definition 10, we determined the desired outcome,\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{\beta}, {\theta }_{\beta}\right),({\mathpzc{q}}_{\beta},{\varphi }_{\beta})\right)\le \left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}^{\prime}}, {\theta }_{{\beta}^{\prime}}\right),({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}^{\prime}},{\varphi }_{{\beta}^{\prime}})\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWA\left({\beta}_{1}, {\beta}_{2}, . . . , {\beta}_{n}\right)\le CFFOWA\left({\beta}_{1}^{\prime}, {\beta}_{2}^{\prime}, . . . , {\beta}_{n}^{\prime}\right).$$\end{document}

In the following, we define CFFOWG operator. Also we analyze its structural properties.

Definition 11.

Let a group consisting of n number of CFFN \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }=\left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }},{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}\right),({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}, {\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }})\right)$$\end{document}, where \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\k{i} =\mathrm{1,2},\dots , n$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\omega ={\left({\omega }_{1}, {\omega }_{2},. . .,{\omega }_{n}\right)}^{T }$$\end{document} be associated weight vector of \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }$$\end{document} with \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\omega }_{\k{i} }\in \left[\mathrm{0,1}\right]$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }=1$$\end{document}, then a complex Fermatean fuzzy order weighted geometric (CFFOWG) operator is a mapping \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWG : {\chi }^{n}\to \chi$$\end{document}, defined by the rule.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWG\left({\beta}_{1}{,\beta}_{2}, . . .,{\beta}_{n}\right)={\otimes }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left(\begin{array}{c}\left({\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}, {\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\right),\\ \left(\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}},\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}\right)\end{array}\right).$$\end{document}

Theorem 5.

Let a group of CFFNs represented as \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }=\left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }},{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}\right),({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}, {\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }})\right)$$\end{document}, where \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\k{i} =\mathrm{1,2},\dots ,n$$\end{document}. The combined outcome of these CFFNs, achieved by the CFFOWG process, remains a CFFN. The expression can be written as:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWG\left({\beta}_{1},{\beta}_{2}, . . .,{\beta}_{n}\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$= \left(\begin{array}{c}\left({\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }},{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\right),\\ \left(\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{q}}_{{F}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}},\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\varphi }_{{F}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}\right)\end{array}\right),$$\end{document}

Where \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\omega = {\left({\omega }_{1}, {\omega }_{2},. . .,{\omega }_{n}\right)}^{T }$$\end{document} represents the weight vector associated with \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }$$\end{document} satisfying conditions \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\omega }_{\k{i} }\in \left[\mathrm{0,1}\right]$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }=1$$\end{document}.

Proof.

Theorem proved through mathematical induction. Suppose that \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$n=2$$\end{document}.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWG\left({\beta}_{1}{,\beta}_{2}\right)={\beta}_{\sigma (1)}^{{\omega }_{1}}\otimes {\beta}_{\sigma (2)}^{{\omega }_{2}}$$\end{document}

Breaking down the components:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\sigma \left(1\right)}^{{\omega }_{1}}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left(\left( {\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(1\right)}}^{{\omega }_{1}},{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(1\right)}}^{{\omega }_{1}}\right),\left(\sqrt[3]{1-{\left(1-{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(1\right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{1}}},\sqrt[3]{1-{\left(1-{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(1\right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{1}}}\right)\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\sigma \left(2\right)}^{{\omega }_{1}}$$\end{document}

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left(\left( {\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(2\right)}}^{{\omega }_{2}},{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(2\right)}}^{{\omega }_{2}}\right),\left(\sqrt[3]{1-{\left(1-{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(1\right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{1}}},\sqrt[3]{1-{\left(1-{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(1\right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{1}}}\right)\right)$$\end{document}

Then,\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\sigma \left(1\right)}^{{\omega }_{1}}\otimes {\beta}_{\sigma \left(2\right)}^{{\omega }_{2}}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left(\left( {\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(1\right)}}^{{\omega }_{1}},{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(1\right)}}^{{\omega }_{1}}\right),\left(\sqrt[3]{1-{\left(1-{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(1\right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{1}}},\sqrt[3]{1-{\left(1-{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(1\right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{1}}}\right)\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\otimes \left(\left( {\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(2\right)}}^{{\omega }_{2}},{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(2\right)}}^{{\omega }_{2}}\right),\left(\sqrt[3]{1-{\left(1-{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(2\right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{2}}},\sqrt[3]{1-{\left(1-{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(2\right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{2}}}\right)\right)$$\end{document}

Consequently,\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWG\left({\beta}_{1},{\beta}_{2}\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left(\begin{array}{c}\left({\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{2}{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }},{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{2}{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\right),\\ \sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{2}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}},\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{2}{\left(1-{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}\end{array}\right)$$\end{document}

Hence, the validity of the theorem holds for \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$n=2$$\end{document}.

Now, let us assume that the theorem holds for \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$n=\kappa>2$$\end{document}, leading to the following:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWG\left({\beta}_{1}{,\beta}_{2}, . . .,{\beta}_{\kappa}\right)={\otimes }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWG\left({\beta}_{1}{,\beta}_{2}, . . .,{\beta}_{\kappa}\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left(\begin{array}{c}\left({\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{\kappa}{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }},{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{\kappa}{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\right),\\ \left(\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{\kappa}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}},\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{\kappa}{\left(1-{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}\right)\end{array}\right)$$\end{document}

Now, for the case \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$n=\kappa+1$$\end{document}, we can express it as:\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWG\left({\beta}_{1}{,\beta}_{2}, . . .,{\beta}_{\kappa},{\beta}_{\kappa+1}\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$={\beta}_{\sigma \left(1\right)}^{{\omega }_{1}}\otimes {\beta}_{\sigma \left(2\right)}^{{\omega }_{2}}\otimes . . . \otimes {\beta}_{\sigma \left(\kappa\right)}^{{\omega }_{\kappa}}\otimes {\beta}_{\sigma \left(\kappa+1\right)}^{{\omega }_{\kappa+1}}$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left(\left({\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{\kappa}{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }},{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{\kappa}{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\right), \left(\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{\kappa}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}},\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{\kappa}{\left(1-{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}\right)\right)$$\end{document}

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\otimes \left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\kappa+1)}}^{{\omega }_{\kappa+1}},{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\kappa+1)}}^{{\omega }_{\kappa+1}}\right), \left(\sqrt[3]{1-{\left(1-{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\kappa+1)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\kappa+1}}},\sqrt[3]{1-{\left(1-{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\kappa+1)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\kappa+1}}}\right)\right)$$\end{document}

This mean that\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWG\left({\beta}_{1}{,\beta}_{2}, . . .,{\beta}_{\kappa+1}\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left(\begin{array}{c}\left({\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{\kappa+1}{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }},{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{\kappa+1}{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\right),\\ \left(\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{\kappa+1}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}},\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{\kappa+1}{\left(1-{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}\right)\end{array}\right)$$\end{document}

This proves the validity of theorem for \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$n=\kappa+1$$\end{document}. Hence, we can deduce that the statement holds for all \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$n\in {\mathbb{Z}}^{+}$$\end{document}.

The utilization of Theorem 5 is illustrated in the given example.

Example 4.

Let \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{1}=\left(\left(\mathrm{0.7,0.4}\right),(\mathrm{0.5,0.6})\right)$$\end{document}, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{2}=\left(\left(\mathrm{0.9,0.5}\right),(\mathrm{0.6,0.8})\right)$$\end{document}, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{3}=\left(\left(\mathrm{0.6,0.7}\right),(\mathrm{0.8,0.4})\right)$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{4}=\left(\left(\mathrm{0.8,0.6}\right),(\mathrm{0.5,0.7})\right)$$\end{document} be CFFNs, and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\omega ={[\mathrm{0.15,0.2,0.25,0.4}]}^{T}$$\end{document} be weight vector of \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }$$\end{document}, where \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\k{i} =\mathrm{1,2},\mathrm{3,4}$$\end{document}. Now we compute score functions of \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }$$\end{document}.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathfrak{H}\left({\beta}_{1}\right)=0.039, \mathfrak{H}\left({\beta}_{2}\right)=0.095$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathfrak{H}\left({\beta}_{3}\right)=0.002, \mathfrak{H}\left({\beta}_{4}\right)=0.167$$\end{document}

Now, we arrange computed values in descending order \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathfrak{H}\left({\beta}_{4}\right)\succ \mathfrak{H}\left({\beta}_{2}\right)\succ \mathfrak{H}\left({\beta}_{1}\right)\succ \mathfrak{H}\left({\beta}_{3}\right)$$\end{document}. Thus\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\sigma (1)}=\left(\left(\mathrm{0.8,0.6}\right),(\mathrm{0.5,0.7})\right),{\beta}_{\sigma (2)}=\left(\left(\mathrm{0.9,0.5}\right),(\mathrm{0.6,0.8})\right),$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\sigma (3)}=\left(\left(\mathrm{0.7,0.4}\right),(\mathrm{0.5,0.6})\right),{\beta}_{\sigma (4)}=\left(\left(\mathrm{0.6,0.7}\right),(\mathrm{0.8,0.4})\right).$$\end{document}

Now\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWG\left({\beta}_{1}{,\beta}_{2},{\beta}_{3},{\beta}_{4}\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left(\begin{array}{c}\left({\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{4}{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }},{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{4}{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\right), \\ \left(\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{4}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}},\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{4}{\left(1-{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}\right)\end{array}\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left(\left(\mathrm{0.70,0.55}\right),\left(\mathrm{0.68,0.63}\right)\right)$$\end{document}

Theorem 6.

(Idempotency)Consider a collection of n number of CFFNs \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }=\left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}, {\theta }_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}\right),({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }},{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }})\right)$$\end{document}, where \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\k{i} =\mathrm{1,2}, \dots , n$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\omega = {\left({\omega }_{1}, {\omega }_{2},. . .,{\omega }_{n}\right)}^{T }$$\end{document} be weight vector associated with \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }$$\end{document}, satisfying the conditions \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\omega }_{\k{i} }\in \left[\mathrm{0,1}\right]$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }=1$$\end{document}. If \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}={\beta}_{\sigma (,j)}$$\end{document} are mathematically identical where \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\sigma (,j)}=\left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (,j)}}, {\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (,j)}}\right),\left({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (,j)}},{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (,j)}}\right)\right)$$\end{document} then,\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWG\left({\beta}_{1}{,\beta}_{2}, . . .,{\beta}_{n}\right)={\beta}_{\sigma (,j)}.$$\end{document}

Proof.

Given that \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}={\beta}_{\sigma (j)}$$\end{document} for all \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\k{i} =\mathrm{1,2},\dots ,n$$\end{document}, and for some \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$j\in \left\{\mathrm{1,2},\dots ,n\right\}$$\end{document} we can deduce that \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}= {\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}$$\end{document}, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}={\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}$$\end{document}, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}= {\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}={\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}$$\end{document}, then by using the above fact in Definition 11, we get.\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWG\left({\beta}_{1}{,\beta}_{2}, . . .,{\beta}_{n}\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$= \left(\begin{array}{c}\left({\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }},{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(\k{i} \right)}}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\right),\\ \left(\sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}, \sqrt[3]{1-{\prod }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\left(1-{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}^{3}\right)}^{{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}\right)\end{array}\right)$$\end{document}\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(j\right)}}^{{\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }},{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(j\right)}}^{{\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }}\right),\left(\sqrt[3]{1-{\left(1-{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(j\right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }}},\sqrt[3]{1-{\left(1-{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(j\right)}}^{3}\right)}^{{\sum }_{\k{i} =1}^{n}{\omega }_{\k{i} }}}\right)\right)$$\end{document}

\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left( \left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(j\right)}}, {\mu }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(j\right)}}\right),\left(\sqrt[3]{1-\left(1-{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(j\right)}}^{3}\right)},\sqrt[3]{1-\left(1-{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(j\right)}}^{3}\right)}\right)\right)$$\end{document} \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$=\left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(j\right)}},{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(j\right)}}\right),\sqrt[3]{{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(j\right)}}^{3}},\sqrt[3]{{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma \left(j\right)}}^{3}}\right)=\left(\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}, {\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}\right),\left({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}},{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (j)}}\right)\right)$$\end{document}

Consequently,\documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$CFFOWG\left({\beta}_{1}{,\beta}_{2}, . . .,{\beta}_{n}\right)={\beta}_{\sigma (j)}$$\end{document}

Theorem 7.

(Boundedness) Let \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}^{-}=\left(\underset{\k{i} }{(\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\},\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\})\underset{\k{i} }{,(\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\}\underset{\k{i} }{,\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\})\right)$$\end{document} and \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}^{+}=\left(\underset{\k{i} }{(\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\},\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{max}}\left\{{\theta }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\})\underset{\k{i} }{,(\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\},\underset{\k{i} }{\mathrm{min}}\left\{{\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\sigma (\k{i} )}}\right\})\right)$$\end{document} be the lower and upper bounds of the CFFNs \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$${\beta}_{\k{i} }=\left({\mathpzc{r}}_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}, {\theta }_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}\right),\left({\mathpzc{q}}_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}, {\varphi }_{{\beta}_{\k{i} }}\right)$$\end{document}, where \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\k{i} =\mathrm{1,2}, \dots ,
